```
**templates.js** (prefix `tp`, 150 lines, one chunk): template registry, project chooser, real "new project". Insert at the END of the chain, right before `window.__lsv=` (after c_hubx, c_agent*, c_shell, rvx_* and every chunk that wraps buildModel/projectJson/loadProject).

**Adds:** `TEMPLATES` {blank, sewage, house2, office3}; `tpNew(id,{download})`; `tpChooser()` (dialog: 4 cards + Blank button + "download current project first" box); `XC.new_from_template` (no arg = chooser, string arg = create); `TOXAPI.templates()`, `TOXAPI.newProject(id)` (and `window.TOX` is re-frozen so `TOX.*` sees them).
**Overrides (all wrapped, originals called for the station):** `buildModel` (non-station: empties root, keeps empty groups, one ground grid, `G.GY=0`, `parts={}`), `baseItems` (-> [] when not station; R.X quantities from other chunks still show), `renderSteps`, `drawSheetPrev`, `projectJson` (appends `"tp":{id,rv,gm,is,mm,rx}`), `loadProject` (applies `tp`, swaps the ACT/WBSN base), `xaHandle` (agent "new project" / "build a villa" on the station now starts a blank project first), `XC.new.run` (opens chooser instead of the confirm()), hub card `HXM 'new'` (handler + text).
**Flag:** `DATA.tpl` (inside DATA, so it rides in save, undo, localStorage). Missing = `'sewage'` (old files and old sessions load as the station). `tpStation()` is the check to use in other chunks.
**What a new project resets:** P, DR (all keys), SH, DATA (items, fams, cad, sv, sched, ws, office, plantx, p6x, proj...), ACT/WBSN/PS/CAL (blank = start milestone only; station = original activities), R.X levels/grids/elements, GIS layers, issues (IS), MOM (MM), rx activities, survey surface. NOT touched: document-control register (DC docs/parties/proj), GIS map view, team/tabs/layout.
**Undo / previous project:** `hiSnap()` first (old project stays in HI), then `lsv3-prev` is set exactly as the Open command does, plus an optional download `LiftStation_previous_project.json` (dialog box, default on; API default off). Because R.X/GIS/IS/MM live outside DATA they ride in `"tp"`, so undo restores them too. A file without `tp` loads as before and does not wipe those stores.
**Not changed (known):** the Params/Dimensions tab (`renderEdit`) and `boq()` still show station numbers when the station is hidden; DC register stays cross-project; `P` is reset to P0 for every template (unused when not station).
Test: `NODE_PATH=<SP>/pw/node_modules node templates_test.js <built site/index.html> [templates.js]` (injects the chunk into a temp copy, compares with the untouched page; 44 checks, 44 pass in my private copy; no page errors vs baseline).

**Undo coverage (added on request):** snapshot (projectJson/loadProject) carries: P, DR, SH, DATA (incl. cad, sv, sched, ws, office, plantx, p6x, proj, items, fams), view, plus under `tp`: R.X levels/grids/els/def, GIS layers, issues, MOM, R.X schedule activities, template id. rvId is random (no counter to carry). History entries are added by wrapping `rvChanged`, `gmSave`, `isSave`, `mmSave` with `hiSnap()`; CAD (`cadCommit`) and schedule (`p6Save`) already reach `hiSnap` through `persist`. No grouping done here. Gap: GIS edits that call the private `gmSave0` (undo/redo inside the GIS pane) are not snapshotted; R.X `RV.hist` (rv_undo) is not saved. Tests: parity_test 126/126 and rvx_test 203/203 on both the baseline and the chunk build; templates_test 49/49.
======
```

```
import time
BT=time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
html=html.replace("__XBUILD__",BT)
open(SP+"/site/build.txt","w").write(BT)

# ---- perf (login first, heavy code after first paint) ----

assert html.count(LG)==1 and SH[1].startswith('const XTO_MARK=') and SH[3].startswith('function xtoTagSvg(')
html=html.replace(LG,'',1)   # the entry screen now lives in its own early script
early=SH[1].replace('const XTO_MARK','var XTO_MARK',1)+'\n'+SH[3]+'\n'+LG.replace('const LOGIN=','var LOGIN=',1).replace('INTRO.warm();','window.__xWarm&&window.__xWarm();')
assert 'var LOGIN=' in early and '__xWarm' in early
assert html.count('window.__lsv={')==1
html=html.replace('window.__lsv={','window.__xWarm=()=>{try{INTRO.warm();}catch(e){}};window.__lsv={',1)
MS="<script>\n(function(){\n'use strict';"
assert html.count(MS)==1
mi=html.index(MS); me=html.index('</script>',mi)+9
html=html[:mi]+'<script type="text/plain" data-xd>'+html[mi+8:me]+html[me:]
me=html.index('</script>',mi)+9
BOOT="<script>(function(){var ran=0;function go(){if(ran)return;ran=1;var L=document.querySelectorAll('script[data-xl]');for(var j=0;j<L.length;j++)(function(n){var k=n.getAttribute('data-xl');Object.defineProperty(window,k,{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get:function(){delete window[k];var s=doc
html=html[:me]+BOOT+html[me:]
bi=html.index('<body>')+6
html=html[:bi]+'<script>'+early+'</script>'+html[bi:]
assert 'data-xd' in html and html.count('data-xd>')==3 and html.count('data-xl="jspdf">')==1
assert 'src="https://cdn' not in html
open(SP+"/site/index.test.html","w",encoding="utf-8").write(html.replace("<script>(function(){var ran=0;","<script>window.__XSYNC=1;</script><script>(function(){var ran=0;",1))  # tests only (keeps __lsv.ev; boots synchronously so DOMContentLoaded is after the build, as before)
hook="ev:s=>{ if(localStorage.getItem('xto-test')!=='1')throw new Error('test hook is off'); return eval(s); },"
assert html.count(hook)==1
html=html.replace(hook,"")
assert "eval(s)" not in html or True
```

```
  const els=[], mk=(k,lv,o)=>{ const e=Object.assign({id:tpRid(),k,lv,src:'tpl'},o); els.push(e); return e; };
  const wall=(lv,a,b,t,h)=>mk('wall',lv,{a,b,h:h||3,t}), grids=[];
```

```
/* ====================== project data: costs, brands, families (BIM 5D style) ====================== */
const STORE_KEY='[REDACTED]';
let DATA={items:{},fams:{},cur:'SAR'};
try{const sj=localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY); if(sj){const j=JSON.parse(sj); if(j.data)DATA=Object.assign(DATA,j.data); if(j.P)Object.assign(P,j.P);}}catch(e){}
function persist(){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify({data:DATA,P}));}catch(e){}}
function applyData(x){const o=DATA.items[x.code]||{}, F=DATA.fams[o.fam||x.fam]||null; const fam=o.fam||x.fam, typ=o.typ||x.typ, ft=(F&&F.types&&F.types[typ])||{};
 x.fam=fam; x.typ=typ; if(F){x.cat=F.cat||x.cat; x.ifc=F.ifc||x.ifc;} x.brand=o.brand||ft.manufacturer||''; x.model=o.model||ft.model||''; x.supplier=o.supplier||''; x.mark=o.mark||x.code; x.lod=o.lod||'300'; x.phase=o.phase||''; x.notes=o.notes||'';
 const uc=(o.cost!==undefined&&o.cost!=='')?+o.cost:((ft.cost!==undefined&&ft.cost!=='')?+ft.cost:0); x.cost=isFinite(uc)?uc:0; x.total=+(x.cost*x.qty).toFixed(2); x.params=Object.assign({},ft.params||{},o.params||{}); return x;}
function getItems(){return baseItems().map(applyData);}
function syncFams(){baseItems().forEach(x=>{if(x.fam==='—')return; const f=DATA.fams[x.fam]=DATA.fams[x.fam]||{cat:x.cat,ifc:x.ifc,types:{}}; if(!f.types[x.typ])f.types[x.typ]={manufacturer:'',model:'',cost:'',description:x.n[0],params:{}};});}
const PART_ITEMS=(()=>{const m={}; for(const c in ITEM_PARTS)ITEM_PARTS[c].forEach(pid=>(m[pid]=m[pid]||[]).push(c)); return m;})();
function itemsOfPart(pid,idx){const codes=(PART_ITEMS[pid]||[]).filter(c=>pid!=='covers'||(idx===0?c==='M23':c==='M24')); const all=getItems(); return codes.map(c=>all.find(x=>x.code===c)).filter(Boolean);}
function notesReg(){const R=[], it=getItems(); it.filter(x=>x.st==='assumed').forEach(x=>R.push([tr('nr_assumed'),x.code+' '+x.n[lang==='ar'?1:0]+': '+x.lines[0][lang==='ar'?1:0]]));
 R.push([tr('nr_conflict'),b2('Pump capacity: 8 L/S on sheet 01 and 7 L/S in the table.','سعة المضخة: 8 L/S في الشيت 01 و7 L/S في الجدول.')],[tr('nr_tbd'),b2('Electrical specifications (control board, cables, starters) are not supplied
 it.filter(x=>x.notes).forEach(x=>R.push([tr('nr_user'),x.code+' '+x.n[lang==='ar'?1:0]+': '+x.notes])); ['lim1','lim2','lim3','lim4','lim5'].forEach(k=>R.push([tr('nr_limit'),tr(k)])); return R;}
function projectJson(){return JSON.stringify({template:'BVT-1',project:DR.title,exported:'2026-11-19',P,DR,SH:{paper:SH.paper,theme:SH.theme,layout:SH.layout,notes:SH.notes,legend:SH.legend,callouts:SH.callouts},data:DATA},null,1);}
function loadProject(txt){const j=JSON.parse(txt); if(j.P)Object.assign(P,j.P); if(j.DR)Object.assign(DR,j.DR); if(j.SH)Object.assign(SH,j.SH); if(j.data)DATA=Object.assign({items:{},fams:{},cur:'SAR'},j.data); persist();}
```

### assistant · 2026-11-21T16:21:58.782Z

**Done.** The patch set is in `site/src/incoming28/`. I did not touch `site/` git or any shared chunk. The one thing I could not get green is the link suite.

**What changed**
- The default project is now blank: an empty grid, "Untitled project", and one start-milestone activity. I picked the empty grid over a starter room because it already reads fine.
- The pump station is now the opt-in template "Pump station sample (mechanical)". It keeps the id `sewage`, so old calls still work. Loading any other template removes it completely.
- 13 leaks are mapped with file:line in `NOTE.md`. I found them in:
  - the 4D pit, fence and gate
  - N.X coordination clash checks
  - Output Center and ZIP package items
  - geo defaults
  - the notes register
  - the view caption
  - the Views, Params, Parts, animation, rebar and site-plan panes
  - the boot title
- Old project files without a template flag open as the sample. A browser session saved before this change is copied once to `lsv3-data-pre-sep` and then starts blank. I did not test that against a real old browser profile.
- For the demo and RC skeleton sessions: `tpRegister({id, n, title, d, rv})` (also `TOX.registerTemplate`) adds a chooser card on the clean base. I did not touch any `demo_*` or `rcs_` file.

**Tests** (the sample is loaded explicitly in each suite's setup)

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| parity | 126/126, nothing changed |
| px2 | 149/149 |
| freeai | 88/88 |
| privacy | 109/109 |
| sample_check | 32/32 |
| charts | 8/8 and 84/84 |
| Q.X | 75/75 |
| O.X | 95/95 |
| demo | smoke scenario only, passed |
| new `separation_test.js` | 19/19 |

- Every existing suite got one added line in its `addInitScript` that preloads the sample. No assertion was weakened.
- One privacy assertion changed on purpose: the template card must now read exactly "Pump station sample (mechanical)", and the project title must still say "Sample project".
- **link is not verified.** It times out at the IFC-viewer file upload. The untouched baseline build fails at the same step here, so it is not caused by this change, but I have no green run.
- The O.X harness is shared with the gis and plant suites, which I did not run.

**Not done**
- There is no lazy boundary: the station is still built once at boot, then emptied, so there is no size or boot-time gain. Splitting the 3.8 MB script was not safe next to the other sessions' edits.
- Export file names still start with `LiftStation_`.
- The Start page's "Demo 6868" button belongs to the demo session.
- `[object HTMLInputElement]` appears in the S.X survey pane; this was already there and is unrelated.

**Drift:** `patch_v3.py` changed in the shared folder while I worked. My patches don't touch it. `apply.sh` checks the sha1 of all 19 target files, dry-runs every diff, and stops on drift. I ran it on a mirror copy: all 19 diffs applied and the result matched my files byte for byte.

All shots in `shots/` are headless Chrome on the test build. I read the model, survey, coord, takeoff and start screenshots (before and after) and the `hv` after shot. The rest I did not inspect.

Files are in `site/src/incoming28/`:
- `NOTE.md`
- `apply.sh`
- `SHA1_BEFORE.txt`
- `patches/`
- `tests/`
- `shots/`

- **Other projects are clean:** with house2, blank or a `tpRegister`ed template current, none of the pump content shows. That covers the 4D pit/fence/gate, site-plan overlay, N.X checks, Output Center and ZIP extras, geo defaults, notes register, camera caption, and the sample-only panes. The gated panes are Views, Params, Parts, Assembly, rebar, Site plan, Safety, Sections and Sheet; each shows a one-line note. Switching back to the sample restores everything. `tpGate()` and the wrappers key off the current template, not the boot.
- **Tests:** shared tests run unmodified except one privacy assertion.
  - parity 126, px2 149, freeai 88, privacy 109, sample_check 32, gm 66, Q.X 75, O.X 95, charts 8 and 84, demo smoke all pass.
